        SELECT jsonb_agg(order_data) FROM (
            SELECT
                o.id AS order_id,
                o.comment AS order_instruction,
                EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (now() - o.created_at)) AS duration_seconds,
                s.name AS section,
                string_agg(t.name, ', ') AS tables,
                (
                    SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                        'order_item_id', oi.id,
                        'item_id', i.id,
                        'item_name', i.name,
                        'quantity', oi.quantity - COALESCE(oi.ready_items_count, 0),
                        'ready_count', COALESCE(oi.ready_items_count, 0),
                        'instruction', oi.comment,
                        'modifiers', (
                            SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                                'modifier_id', m.id,
                                'modifier_name', m.name
                            ))
                            FROM order_modifiers om
                            JOIN modifiers m ON m.id = om.modifier_id
                            WHERE om.order_item_id = oi.id AND om.is_deleted = false
                        )
                    ))
                    FROM order_items oi
                    JOIN items i ON i.id = oi.item_id
                    WHERE oi.order_id = o.id AND oi.is_deleted = false AND (oi.quantity - COALESCE(oi.ready_items_count, 0)) > 0
                ) AS items
            FROM orders o
            JOIN order_table_mappings otm ON otm.order_id = o.id AND otm.is_deleted = false
            JOIN tables t ON t.id = otm.table_id
            JOIN sections s ON s.id = t.section_id
            WHERE o.is_deleted = false
                AND o.status != 'Completed'
                AND (p_order_id IS NULL OR o.id = p_order_id)
            GROUP BY o.id, o.comment, o.created_at, s.name
            ORDER BY o.id
            OFFSET (p_page_index - 1) * p_page_size
            LIMIT p_page_size
        ) AS order_data
    )
